4f:T217e,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> ExpectedArgumentCount takes no arguments and returns a non-negative <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#integer">integer</a></emu-xref>. It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-FormalParameters" name="FormalParameters"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-constraints>[empty]</emu-constraints></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionRestParameter">FunctionRestParameter</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return 0.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-FormalParameters" name="FormalParameters" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameterList">FormalParameterList</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>,</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionRestParameter">FunctionRestParameter</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">Return the ExpectedArgumentCount of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameterList">FormalParameterList</a></emu-nt>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The ExpectedArgumentCount of a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameterList">FormalParameterList</a></emu-nt> is the number of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> to the left of either the rest parameter or the first <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameter">FormalParameter</a></emu-nt> with an Initializer. A <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameter">FormalParameter</a></emu-nt> without an initializer is allowed after the first parameter with an initializer but such parameters are considered to be optional with <emu-val>undefined</emu-val> as their default value.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

    <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>The syntactic context immediately following <code>yield</code> requires use of the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#prod-InputElementRegExpOrTemplateTail">InputElementRegExpOrTemplateTail</a></emu-nt> lexical goal.</p>
    </div></emu-note>
    <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-YieldExpression">YieldExpression</a></emu-nt> cannot be used within the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> of a generator function because any expressions that are part of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> are evaluated before the resulting Generator is in a resumable state.</p>
    </div></emu-note>
    <emu-note data-num="3"><span class="note">Note 3</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-abstract-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-abstract-operations">Abstract operations</a></emu-xref> relating to Generators are defined in <emu-xref href="#sec-generator-abstract-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/control-abstraction-objects#sec-generator-abstract-operations">27.5.3</a></emu-xref>.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

<emu-production id="prod-AsyncFunctionBody" name="AsyncFunctionBody"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionBody">AsyncFunctionBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[~Yield, +Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-AwaitExpression" name="AwaitExpression"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AwaitExpression">AwaitExpression</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[Yield]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>await</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-UnaryExpression">UnaryExpression</a><emu-mods><emu-params>[?Yield, +Await]</emu-params></emu-mods></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>

    <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p><code>await</code> is parsed as a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#sec-keywords-and-reserved-words"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-lexical-grammar#sec-keywords-and-reserved-words">keyword</a></emu-xref> of an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AwaitExpression">AwaitExpression</a></emu-nt> when the <sub>[Await]</sub> parameter is present. The <sub>[Await]</sub> parameter is present in the top level of the following contexts, although the parameter may be absent in some contexts depending on the nonterminals, such as <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt>:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>In an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionBody">AsyncFunctionBody</a></emu-nt>.</li>
        <li>In the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> of an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionDeclaration">AsyncFunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionExpression">AsyncFunctionExpression</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorDeclaration">AsyncGeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, or <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorExpression">AsyncGeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt>. <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AwaitExpression">AwaitExpression</a></emu-nt> in this position is a Syntax error via <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-static-semantic-rules"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-static-semantic-rules">static semantics</a></emu-xref>.</li>
        <li>In a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Module">Module</a></emu-nt>.</li>
      </ul>
      <p>When <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules#prod-Script">Script</a></emu-nt> is the syntactic <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-context-free-grammars"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-context-free-grammars">goal symbol</a></emu-xref>, <code>await</code> may be parsed as an identifier when the <sub>[Await]</sub> parameter is absent. This includes the following contexts:</p>
      <ul>
        <li>Anywhere outside of an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionBody">AsyncFunctionBody</a></emu-nt> or <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> of an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionDeclaration">AsyncFunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionExpression">AsyncFunctionExpression</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorDeclaration">AsyncGeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, or <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorExpression">AsyncGeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt>.</li>
        <li>In the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> of a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionExpression">FunctionExpression</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorExpression">GeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt>, or <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorExpression">AsyncGeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt>.</li>
      </ul>
    </div></emu-note>

    <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
      <p>Unlike <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-YieldExpression">YieldExpression</a></emu-nt>, it is a Syntax Error to omit the operand of an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AwaitExpression">AwaitExpression</a></emu-nt>. You must await something.</p>
    </div></emu-note>

8c:T116c,<p>The abstract operation IsInTailPosition takes argument <var>call</var> (a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CallExpression">CallExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>, a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-MemberExpression">MemberExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>, or an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-OptionalChain">OptionalChain</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>) and returns a Boolean. It performs the following steps when called:</p>
      <emu-alg><ol><li class="exit">If <emu-xref aoid="IsStrict"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-isstrict">IsStrict</a></emu-xref>(<var>call</var>) is <emu-val>false</emu-val>, return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">If <var>call</var> is not contained within a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ConciseBody">ConciseBody</a></emu-nt>, or an <emu-nt><a <var>body</var> is the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> of a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorBody">GeneratorBody</a></emu-nt>, return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">If <var>body</var> is the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> of an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionBody">AsyncFunctionBody</a></emu-nt>, return <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">If <var>body</var> is <var>call</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

8f:T2838,<emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-FunctionDeclaration" name="FunctionDeclaration"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production>

<emu-production id="prod-FunctionExpression" name="FunctionExpression"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionExpression">FunctionExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <ul>
        <li>
          If <emu-xref aoid="IsStrict"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-isstrict">IsStrict</a></emu-xref>(<emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, the Early Error rules for <emu-grammar><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-UniqueFormalParameters">UniqueFormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt></emu-grammar> are applied.
        </li>
        <li>
          If <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> is present and <emu-xref aoid="IsStrict"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-isstrict">IsStrict</a></emu-xref>(<emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, it is a Syntax Error if the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> is either <emu-val>"eval"</emu-val> or <emu-val>"arguments"</emu-val>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if <emu-xref aoid="FunctionBodyContainsUseStrict"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#sec-static-semantics-functionbodycontainsusestrict">FunctionBodyContainsUseStrict</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>true</emu-val> and <emu-xref aoid="IsSimpleParameterList"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#sec-static-semantics-issimpleparameterlist">IsSimpleParameterList</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>false</emu-val>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if any element of the <emu-xref aoid="BoundNames"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-boundnames">BoundNames</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> also occurs in the <emu-xref aoid="LexicallyDeclaredNames"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-lexicallydeclarednames">LexicallyDeclaredNames</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref aoid="Contains"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-contains">Contains</a></emu-xref> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-SuperProperty">SuperProperty</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref aoid="Contains"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-contains">Contains</a></emu-xref> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-SuperProperty">SuperProperty</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.

        <li>

        </li>
        <li>

      </ul>

      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> in a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionExpression">FunctionExpression</a></emu-nt> can be referenced from inside the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionExpression">FunctionExpression</a></emu-nt>'s <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> to allow the function to call itself recursively. However, unlike in a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> in a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionExpression">FunctionExpression</a></emu-nt> cannot be referenced from and does not affect the scope enclosing the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionExpression">FunctionExpression</a></emu-nt>.</p>

98:T1768,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> InstantiateArrowFunctionExpression takes optional argument <var>name</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property key</a></emu-xref> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names">Private Name</a></emu-xref>) and returns an ECMAScript <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref>. It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ArrowFunction" name="ArrowFunction" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowParameters">ArrowParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>=></emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ConciseBody">ConciseBody</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>If <var>name</var> is not present, set <var>name</var> to the empty String.</li><li>Let <var>envRecord</var> be the LexicalEnvironment of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref>.</li><li>Let <var>privateEnv</var> be the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref>'s PrivateEnvironment.</li><li>Let <var>sourceText</var> be the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">source text matched by</a></emu-xref> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt>.</li><li id="step-arrowfunction-evaluation-functioncreate">Let <var>closure</var> be <emu-xref aoid="OrdinaryFunctionCreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinaryfunctioncreate">OrdinaryFunctionCreate</a></emu-xref>(<emu-intrinsic>%Function.prototype%</emu-intrinsic>, <var>sourceText</var>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowParameters">ArrowParameters</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ConciseBody">ConciseBody</a></emu-nt>, <emu-const>lexical-this</emu-const>, <var>envRecord</var>, <var>privateEnv</var>).</li><li>Perform <emu-xref aoid="SetFunctionName"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-setfunctionname">SetFunctionName</a></emu-xref>(<var>closure</var>, <var>name</var>).</li><li class="exit">Return <var>closure</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>An <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> does not define local bindings for <code>arguments</code>, <code>super</code>, <code>this</code>, or <code>new.target</code>. Any reference to <code>arguments</code>, <code>super</code>, <code>this</code>, or <code>new.target</code> within an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> must resolve to a binding in a lexically enclosing environment. Typically this will be the Function Environment of an immediately enclosing function. Even though an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> may contain references to <code>super</code>, the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> created in step <emu-xref href="#step-arrowfunction-evaluation-functioncreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#step-arrowfunction-evaluation-functioncreate">5</a></emu-xref> is not made into a method by performing <emu-xref aoid="MakeMethod"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-makemethod">MakeMethod</a></emu-xref>. An <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> that references <code>super</code> is always contained within a non-<emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> and the necessary state to implement <code>super</code> is accessible via the <var>envRecord</var> that is captured by the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> of the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ArrowFunction">ArrowFunction</a></emu-nt>.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if <emu-xref aoid="HasDirectSuper"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#sec-static-semantics-hasdirectsuper">HasDirectSuper</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorMethod">GeneratorMethod</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.

<emu-production id="prod-GeneratorExpression" name="GeneratorExpression"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorExpression">GeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>function</emu-t> <emu-t>*</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorBody">GeneratorBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <ul>
        <li>
          If <emu-xref aoid="IsStrict"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-isstrict">IsStrict</a></emu-xref>(<emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, the Early Error rules for <emu-grammar><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-UniqueFormalParameters">UniqueFormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt></emu-grammar> are applied.
        </li>
        <li>
          If <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> is present and <emu-xref aoid="IsStrict"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-isstrict">IsStrict</a></emu-xref>(<emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, it is a Syntax Error if the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> is either <emu-val>"eval"</emu-val> or <emu-val>"arguments"</emu-val>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if <emu-xref aoid="FunctionBodyContainsUseStrict"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#sec-static-semantics-functionbodycontainsusestrict">FunctionBodyContainsUseStrict</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorBody">GeneratorBody</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>true</emu-val> and <emu-xref aoid="IsSimpleParameterList"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#sec-static-semantics-issimpleparameterlist">IsSimpleParameterList</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>false</emu-val>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if any element of the <emu-xref aoid="BoundNames"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-boundnames">BoundNames</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> also occurs in the <emu-xref aoid="LexicallyDeclaredNames"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-lexicallydeclarednames">LexicallyDeclaredNames</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorBody">GeneratorBody</a></emu-nt>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref aoid="Contains"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-contains">Contains</a></emu-xref> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-YieldExpression">YieldExpression</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref aoid="Contains"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-contains">Contains</a></emu-xref> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-SuperProperty">SuperProperty</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorBody">GeneratorBody</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref aoid="Contains"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-contains">Contains</a></emu-xref> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-SuperProperty">SuperProperty</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.
        </li>
        <li>
          It is a Syntax Error if <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref aoid="Contains"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/syntax-directed-operations#sec-static-semantics-contains">Contains</a></emu-xref> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-SuperCall">SuperCall</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>.
        </li>

        </li>

      <emu-alg><ol><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: <var>name</var> is not present.</li><li>Set <var>name</var> to the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt>.</li><li>Let <var>outerEnv</var> be the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref>'s LexicalEnvironment.</li><li>Let <var>funcEnv</var> be <emu-xref aoid="NewDeclarativeEnvironment"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-newdeclarativeenvironment">NewDeclarativeEnvironment</a></emu-xref>(<var>outerEnv</var>).</li><li>Perform ! <var>funcEnv</var>.CreateImmutableBinding(<var>name</var>, <emu-val>false</emu-val>).</li><li>Let <var>privateEnv</var> be the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref>'s PrivateEnvironment.</li><li>Let <var>sourceText</var> be the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">source text matched by</a></emu-xref> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorExpression">GeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt>.</li><li>Let <var>closure</var> be <emu-xref aoid="OrdinaryFunctionCreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinaryfunctioncreate">OrdinaryFunctionCreate</a></emu-xref>(<emu-intrinsic>%GeneratorFunction.prototype%</emu-intrinsic>, <var>sourceText</var>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt>, <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorBody">GeneratorBody</a></emu-nt>, <emu-const>non-lexical-this</emu-const>, <var>funcEnv</var>, <var>privateEnv</var>).</li><li>Perform <emu-xref aoid="SetFunctionName"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-setfunctionname">SetFunctionName</a></emu-xref>(<var>closure</var>, <var>name</var>).</li><li>Let <var>proto</var> be <emu-xref aoid="OrdinaryObjectCreate"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours#sec-ordinaryobjectcreate">OrdinaryObjectCreate</a></emu-xref>(<emu-intrinsic>%GeneratorPrototype%</emu-intrinsic>).</li><li>Perform ! <var>funcEnv</var>.InitializeBinding(<var>name</var>, <var>closure</var>).</li><li class="exit">Return <var>closure</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> in a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorExpression">GeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt> can be referenced from inside the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorExpression">GeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt>'s <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionBody">FunctionBody</a></emu-nt> to allow the generator code to call itself recursively. However, unlike in a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorDeclaration">GeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> in a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorExpression">GeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt> cannot be referenced from and does not affect the scope enclosing the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-GeneratorExpression">GeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt>.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> in an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorExpression">AsyncGeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt> can be referenced from inside the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorExpression">AsyncGeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt>'s <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorBody">AsyncGeneratorBody</a></emu-nt> to allow the generator code to call itself recursively. However, unlike in an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorDeclaration">AsyncGeneratorDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> in an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorExpression">AsyncGeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt> cannot be referenced from and does not affect the scope enclosing the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncGeneratorExpression">AsyncGeneratorExpression</a></emu-nt>.</p>

b6:T767d,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> ClassDefinitionEvaluation takes arguments <var>classBinding</var> (a String or <emu-val>undefined</emu-val>), <var>className</var> (a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#property-key">property key</a></emu-xref> or a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names">Private Name</a></emu-xref>), and <var>sourceText</var> (<emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-source-text"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-source-text">ECMAScript source text</a></emu-xref>) and returns either a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">normal completion containing</a></emu-xref> a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#function-object">function object</a></emu-xref> or an <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-completion-record-specification-type">abrupt completion</a></emu-xref>.</p>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>For ease of specification, private methods and accessors are included alongside private fields in the <var class="field">[[PrivateElements]]</var> slot of class instances. However, any given object has either all or none of the private methods and accessors defined by a given class. This feature has been designed so that implementations may choose to implement private methods and accessors using a strategy which does not require tracking each method or accessor individually.</p>
        <p>For example, an implementation could directly associate instance private methods with their corresponding <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names">Private Name</a></emu-xref> and track, for each object, which class <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor">constructors</a></emu-xref> have run with that object as their <code>this</code> value. Looking up an instance private method on an object then consists of checking that the class <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#constructor">constructor</a></emu-xref> which defines the method has been used to initialize the object, then returning the method associated with the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-private-names">Private Name</a></emu-xref>.</p>
        <p>This differs from private fields: because field initializers can throw during class instantiation, an individual object may have some proper subset of the private fields of a given class, and so private fields must in general be tracked individually.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <p>It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
<emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ClassTail" name="ClassTail" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassTail">ClassTail</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassHeritage">ClassHeritage</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>{</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-ClassBody">ClassBody</a><emu-mods><emu-opt>opt</emu-opt></emu-mods></emu-nt> <emu-t>}</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>envRecord</var> be the LexicalEnvironment of the <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#running-execution-context">running execution context</a></emu-xref>.</li><li>Let <var>classEnv</var> be <emu-xref aoid="NewDeclarativeEnvironment"><a         <li>It is a Syntax Error if <emu-xref aoid="FunctionBodyContainsUseStrict"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#sec-static-semantics-functionbodycontainsusestrict">FunctionBodyContainsUseStrict</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionBody">AsyncFunctionBody</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>true</emu-val> and <emu-xref aoid="IsSimpleParameterList"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#sec-static-semantics-issimpleparameterlist">IsSimpleParameterList</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FormalParameters">FormalParameters</a></emu-nt> is <emu-val>false</emu-val>.</li>

        <li>If <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> is present and <emu-xref aoid="IsStrict"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-source-code#sec-isstrict">IsStrict</a></emu-xref>(<emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt>) is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, it is a Syntax Error if the <emu-xref aoid="StringValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue">StringValue</a></emu-xref> of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> is either <emu-val>"eval"</emu-val> or <emu-val>"arguments"</emu-val>.</li>

      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>The <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> in an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionExpression">AsyncFunctionExpression</a></emu-nt> can be referenced from inside the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionExpression">AsyncFunctionExpression</a></emu-nt>'s <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionBody">AsyncFunctionBody</a></emu-nt> to allow the function to call itself recursively. However, unlike in a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-FunctionDeclaration">FunctionDeclaration</a></emu-nt>, the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-BindingIdentifier">BindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> in a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionExpression">AsyncFunctionExpression</a></emu-nt> cannot be referenced from and does not affect the scope enclosing the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncFunctionExpression">AsyncFunctionExpression</a></emu-nt>.</p>

c2:T596,<emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-AsyncArrowFunction" name="AsyncArrowFunction"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncArrowFunction">AsyncArrowFunction</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>async</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncArrowBindingIdentifier">AsyncArrowBindingIdentifier</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>=></emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncConciseBody">AsyncConciseBody</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs>  <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-CoverCallExpressionAndAsyncArrowHead">CoverCallExpressionAndAsyncArrowHead</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>=></emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes#prod-AsyncConciseBody">AsyncConciseBody</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>

    8b:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-async-arrow-function-definitions-runtime-semantics-evaluation","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"1595-runtime-semantics-evaluation","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"15.9.5"}]," Runtime Semantics: Evaluation"],["$","$L38",null,{"id":"1595-runtime-semantics-evaluation"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$c2"}}]]}]
c3:Te311,<p>The <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations">syntax-directed operation</a></emu-xref> HasCallInTailPosition takes argument <var>call</var> (a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CallExpression">CallExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>, a <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-MemberExpression">MemberExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>, or an <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-OptionalChain">OptionalChain</a></emu-nt> <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>) and returns a Boolean.</p>
      <emu-note data-num="1"><span class="note">Note 1</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p><var>call</var> is a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref> that represents a specific range of source text. When the following algorithms compare <var>call</var> to another <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">Parse Node</a></emu-xref>, it is a test of whether they represent the same source text.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
      <emu-note data-num="2"><span class="note">Note 2</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>A potential tail position call that is immediately followed by return <emu-xref aoid="GetValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-getvalue">GetValue</a></emu-xref> of the call result is also a possible tail position call. A function call cannot return a <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-reference-record-specification-type"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-reference-record-specification-type">Reference Record</a></emu-xref>, so such a <emu-xref aoid="GetValue"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-data-types-and-values#sec-getvalue">GetValue</a></emu-xref> operation will always return the same value as the actual function call result.</p>
      </div></emu-note>

      <p>It is defined piecewise over the following productions:</p>
<emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-StatementList" name="StatementList" collapsed=""><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementList">StatementList</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq> <emu-rhs><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementList">StatementList</a></emu-nt> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementListItem">StatementListItem</a></emu-nt></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>
      <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>has</var> be HasCallInTailPosition of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementList">StatementList</a></emu-nt> with argument <var>call</var>.</li><li class="exit">If <var>has</var> is <emu-val>true</emu-val>, return <emu-val>true</emu-val>.</li><li class="exit">Return HasCallInTailPosition of <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-statements-and-declarations#prod-StatementListItem">StatementListItem</a></emu-nt> with argument <var>call</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>

      <emu-alg><ol><li>Let <var>expr</var> be the <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ParenthesizedExpression">ParenthesizedExpression</a></emu-nt> that is <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#sec-syntactic-grammar">covered</a></emu-xref> by <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList">CoverParenthesizedExpressionAndArrowParameterList</a></emu-nt>.</li><li class="exit">Return HasCallInTailPosition of <var>expr</var> with argument <var>call</var>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <emu-grammar type="definition"><emu-production id="prod-ParenthesizedExpression" name="ParenthesizedExpression"><emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-ParenthesizedExpression">ParenthesizedExpression</a></emu-nt> <emu-geq>:</emu-geq>  <emu-rhs><emu-t>(</emu-t> <emu-nt><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/ecmascript-language-expressions#prod-Expression">Expression</a></emu-nt> <emu-t>)</emu-t></emu-rhs></emu-production></emu-grammar>

    8d:["$","emu-clause",null,{"id":"sec-static-semantics-hascallintailposition","children":[["$","h1",null,{"id":"15102-static-semantics-hascallintailposition","className":"x:tracking-tight x:text-slate-900 x:dark:text-slate-100 x:font-bold x:mt-2 x:text-4xl","children":[[["$","span",null,{"className":"secnum","children":"15.10.2"}]," Static Semantics: HasCallInTailPosition"],["$","$L38",null,{"id":"15102-static-semantics-hascallintailposition"}]]}],["$","div",null,{"dangerouslySetInnerHTML":{"__html":"$c3"}}]]}]
c4:T8f9,<p>The abstract operation PrepareForTailCall takes no arguments and returns <emu-const>unused</emu-const>. It performs the following steps when called:</p>
      <emu-alg><ol><li><emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/notational-conventions#assert">Assert</a></emu-xref>: The current <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref> will not subsequently be used for the evaluation of any ECMAScript code or built-in functions. The invocation of Call subsequent to the invocation of this abstract operation will create and push a new <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref> before performing any such evaluation.</li><li>Discard all resources associated with the current <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref>.</li><li class="exit">Return <emu-const>unused</emu-const>.</li></ol></emu-alg>
      <p>A tail position call must either release any transient internal resources associated with the currently executing function <emu-xref href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts"><a href="/ecma262/nextra-poc/executable-code-and-execution-contexts#sec-execution-contexts">execution context</a></emu-xref> before invoking the target function or reuse those resources in support of the target function.</p>
      <emu-note><span class="note">Note</span><div class="note-contents">
        <p>For example, a tail position call should only grow an implementation's activation record stack by the amount that the size of the target function's activation record exceeds the size of the calling function's activation record. If the target function's activation record is smaller, then the total size of the stack should decrease.</p>
      </div></emu-note>
